## Best date plans

### 2026-10-11 — Catch the Colors: Fall at Green-Wood + Terre

**Catch the Colors: Fall at Green-Wood, then a meal.** Italian at Terre. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=500+25th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11232&destination=341+5th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.green-wood.com/event/catch-the-colors-fall-at-green-wood-2/2026-10-11/) · [Restaurant](https://www.terre-brooklyn.com/)

### 2026-10-13 — Terre + Jens Lekman

**Dinner, then Jens Lekman.** Italian at Terre. It is a 10-minute walk from the venue. Jens Lekman starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=341+5th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=233+Butler+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://publicrecords.nyc/calendar) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43363115-jens-lekman-at-public-records) · [Restaurant](https://www.terre-brooklyn.com/)

### 2026-10-08 — Good Time Country Buffet + Neko Case

**Dinner, then Neko Case.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Neko Case starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=196+Avenue+A%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&destination=80+N+6th+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nationalsawdust.org/) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43409441-neko-case-at-national-sawdust) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/)

### 2026-10-08 — Sense + the City opening reception + Good Time Country Buffet

**Sense + the City opening reception, then dinner.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=56+East+1st+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&destination=196+Avenue+A%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://citylore.org/multisensory-a-sense-the-city-exhibit/) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/)

**Dinner, then School Girls; Or, The African Mean Girls Play.** French at Cafe Charmant. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. School Girls; Or, The African Mean Girls Play starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

### 2026-10-09 — Rolo's + Great Lake Swimmers with Colleen Brown

**Dinner, then Great Lake Swimmers with Colleen Brown.** New American at Rolo's. It is a 10-minute walk from the venue. Great Lake Swimmers with Colleen Brown starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 47.6  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.
[Official](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

## Where to eat

Verified against captured restaurant pages, menu text/PDF extracts, Eater/Resy reporting, and a limited set of official follow-up pages for high-risk opening and menu claims.

Dining research: 2026-10-08T10:35:41+00:00

No Michelin recognition is used as a recommendation or award claim for these candidates. Resy describes Eleven Madison Park as Daniel Humm's three-Michelin-starred restaurant, but that is background about EMP, not a star claim for Humm. No candidate is retained with an unsourced Michelin-star or Michelin Guide-selection claim.

### Daily $20 happy-hour combo at Hutong New York

Deals & happy hours — Hutong New York

Official menu lists a daily happy hour with one selected drink and one food item for $20 per person, useful for a Midtown high-end Chinese option before dinner.

Price: $20 per person combo; individual happy-hour drinks $6-$12 and snacks $12-$18 also listed  
Schedule: Daily from 12pm-7pm  
Conditions: Combo is select one drink and one food item of choice; tax, tip, and service charge not stated; reservation link provided but reservation requirement not explicitly stated.  
[Source](https://www.hutong-nyc.com/menus/happy-hour/)

### $38 weekday lunch prix fixe at Hutong New York

Deals & happy hours — Hutong New York

Three-part official lunch menu offers appetizer, main, and side with choices such as kou shui chicken, mapo tofu, steamed red snapper, kung po chicken, and chef's fried rice.

Price: $38 per person; optional sommelier wine pairing $28 per person; supplements include seared scallop +$10, roasted Peking duck +$30 per person, ma la beef tenderloin +$22  
Schedule: Monday-Friday from 12pm-4pm  
Conditions: Select one appetizer, one main, and one side; supplements extra; allergy notice on menu; tax, tip, and service charge not stated.  
[Source](https://www.hutong-nyc.com/menus/set-lunch-menu/)

### Weekend unlimited dim sum brunch at Hutong New York

Deals & happy hours — Hutong New York

Official brunch menu offers unlimited dim sum with items including vegetarian spring rolls, spinach dumplings, prawn rolls, pork dumplings, chicken wontons, and optional Peking duck add-on.

Price: Unlimited dim sum $48 per person; 90 minutes bottomless bubbles and mimosas +$38 per person; 90 minutes bottomless Moet & Chandon Champagne +$78 per person; Peking duck add-on $30 per guest  
Schedule: Saturday-Sunday, 11:30am-4pm  
Conditions: Unlimited dim sum to be enjoyed by the entire table; 90-minute limit applies to bottomless beverage options; some dim sum have +$18 supplements; tax, tip, and service charge not stated.  
[Source](https://www.hutong-nyc.com/menus/bubbles-bao-brunch-menu/)

### Tale of Mahjong cocktail menu at Hutong New York

Special menus — Hutong New York

Official special cocktail menu is organized around Mahjong tiles with 0% ABV drinks at $16 and alcoholic cocktails mostly $22-$28, useful for a themed bar stop rather than a full dinner.

Price: 0% ABV cocktails $16; alcoholic cocktails $22-$28  
Schedule: Daily  
Conditions: Food purchase, seating area, tax, tip, and service charge not stated; reservation link provided.  
[Source](https://www.hutong-nyc.com/menus/tale-of-mahjong/)

Andrew Rannells, Megan Hilty, Tituss Burgess, Harry Hamlin, and Lisa Rinna star in Joe Landry's live-radio-play staging of the holiday story.

#### [Downstate](https://www.downstateonbroadway.com/)

2026-10-17 · First preview October 17; opening November 15; closing January 10, 2027 · theater · Varies · Booth Theatre · Times Square · Manhattan · Score 57.0  
Bruce Norris's Downstate comes to Broadway in a strictly limited 12-week engagement directed by Joe Mantello, following a confrontation in a state-sanctioned group home.
**Practical note:** Restricted to audiences 17 and older due to mature content.
**Why now:** The official Broadway listing confirms a finite run ending January 10.
**Book soon:** Broadway.org describes this as a strictly limited 12-week engagement and lists January 10, 2027 as the closing date.
